# Roth/Index.lean / Roth/Taylor.lean

418 comparisons among the 293 accepted declarations. [Source hashes and review provenance](Index--Taylor.json).

| Declaration A | Declaration B | Same statement? | Same proof / mathematical relationship? | Recommended action |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Roth.index` | `MvPolynomial.taylor` | no | Translation returns an entire polynomial algebra endomorphism; index returns a real infimum of weights on one translated support. The latter genuinely depends on the former but discards almost all coefficient information. | Keep semantic polynomial order separate from translation, with weightedOrder representation evaluated in the accepted index report. |
| `Roth.mem_support_taylor_iff` | `MvPolynomial.taylor` | no | A: A Taylor coefficient is supported exactly when the corresponding Hasse derivative evaluates nonzero at the center. Mechanism: Combines mem_support_iff with the project coeff_taylor identity. B: Defines multivariate polynomial translation by a vector as a bundled coefficient-algebra endomorphism. Mechanism: The universal evaluation homomorphism aeval substitutes X j+C(α j) for each variable, automatically preserving sums, products, and coefficients. Index is defined from the support of Taylor translation. Exact translation identities and coefficient/Hasse correspondence are representation inputs; taking a finite weighted infimum adds a separate nonempty/attainment/order layer. Ring inverse translation establishes nonzero support for P!=0; a generic semiring translation need not be injective. A support minimum cannot reconstruct the translated polynomial or its coefficients. | Share translation/support transport and finite-extremum primitives; retain distinct support computation, invertibility and minimum-order interfaces. |
| `Roth.index_le_weight` | `MvPolynomial.taylor` | no | A: The index is at most the weight of any Hasse derivative nonvanishing at the point. Mechanism: Applies csInf_le to the finite image support after mem_support_taylor_iff establishes membership. B: Defines multivariate polynomial translation by a vector as a bundled coefficient-algebra endomorphism. Mechanism: The universal evaluation homomorphism aeval substitutes X j+C(α j) for each variable, automatically preserving sums, products, and coefficients. Index is defined from the support of Taylor translation. Exact translation identities and coefficient/Hasse correspondence are representation inputs; taking a finite weighted infimum adds a separate nonempty/attainment/order layer. Ring inverse translation establishes nonzero support for P!=0; a generic semiring translation need not be injective. A support minimum cannot reconstruct the translated polynomial or its coefficients. | Share translation/support transport and finite-extremum primitives; retain distinct support computation, invertibility and minimum-order interfaces. |
| `Roth.exists_weight_eq_index` | `MvPolynomial.taylor` | no | A: For a nonzero polynomial, some nonvanishing Hasse derivative attains its index. B: Defines multivariate polynomial translation by a vector as a bundled coefficient-algebra endomorphism. B: Defines multivariate polynomial translation by a vector as a bundled coefficient-algebra endomorphism.
